# Banuba

> Banuba provides advanced AR and AI SDKs — including Camera SDK, Video Editor SDK, Virtual Try-On, and WebAR — empowering developers to build immersive, privacy-first visual experiences for mobile, web, and enterprise platforms.

This index lists Banuba's canonical product, documentation, community, and media resources formatted for LLM consumption. Links are grouped into sections; use the Optional section for secondary or archival resources that an LLM may skip when brevity is required.
